# Changelog

Every departure from the source rules, newest first. One line each, with links to the Rangoric note and its source.

| Date | Change | Rangoric note | Source | Why |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 2026-11-28 | Faeries' names come back where earlier imports took them out (FX 1): Liorah the Crystal-keeper, Alden the Keeper of Doors and Burakh, Guvendi the Doorway Guardian, King Eylwor and Queen Garilil, King Kerigan "Goldentouch", King Dubgaradh, King Griakolg, King Mnish, Huerick and Silkirk of the Millhouse, Zurlein and Zurlein's Boon, Merle of Endswick's quotes and asides, and the bogie names. Eon of Sleep is Zurlein's Eon of Sleep again, and three items take their names back: Pine Needle Armor of Anadahrl, Scurry's Dampening Mushroom Armor, Boots of the Valley of Fire. Kokopelli joins the personalities, and Improved Familiar lets a standard familiar take the fey-born template. | [[rangoric-srd/flavor/the-fey-realm/personalities/Kokopelli\|Kokopelli]], [[rangoric-srd/spells/Zurlein’s Eon of Sleep\|Zurlein’s Eon of Sleep]], [[rangoric-srd/feats/Improved Familiar\|Improved Familiar]] (see [[FX Batch 1 Changes]]) | | [[Faeries Fixes]] FX 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | The jabberwocks' numbers are worked out from their Hit Dice and ability scores: the lesser jabberwock's attacks become +9 (claws) and +7 (bite, tail slam, wing slaps) and its Fortitude Defense 18; the greater jabberwock's attacks become +23 (claws) and +21 (bite, tail slam), its Defenses Fort 26, Ref 21, Will 23, and its acidic bite +14 vs Fortitude. | [[rangoric-srd/monsters/jabberwock/Lesser Jabberwock\|Lesser Jabberwock]], [[rangoric-srd/monsters/jabberwock/Greater Jabberwock\|Greater Jabberwock]] | | Faeries fixes |
| 2026-11-28 | Monster letter lists as bases (LB 1): the 14 hand-kept monster lists (All Monsters and Monsters A to Monsters T to Z) are gone, since they had drifted from the monster notes. Monsters now shows the Monsters Index base under "In Alphabetical Order", as Animals does, and the base's new "By group" view lists each family's members under it. The 16 links into the lists now point to what they meant: earth elementals, plant traits, standard dragons, dretches, the succubus, gelugons, lycanthropes, fiendish and celestial creatures, elementals, the elasmosaurus and the tyrannosaurus. "Check notes" fails a hand-kept monster list if one comes back. | [[rangoric-srd/monsters/Monsters\|Monsters]] (see [[LB Batch 1 Changes]]) | | [[Monster Letter Lists as Bases]] LB 1 |
| 2026-11-28 | The staff of spiders and the animate staff, held from IT 1 (Items Part 2 IT 1b): two recharging staves in `magic-items/staffs`. The staff of spiders' "giant spiders" are the [[rangoric-srd/monsters/monstrous-spider/Monstrous Spider, Medium\|Medium monstrous spider]]; the animate staff's "Medium animated object with the metal special ability" becomes a Medium animated object "made of metal (hardness 10 and +2 natural armor)", since Rangoric's animated object has no construction points. | [[rangoric-srd/magic-items/staffs/Staff of Spiders\|Staff of Spiders]], [[rangoric-srd/magic-items/staffs/Animate Staff\|Animate Staff]] | | Staff of Spiders and Animate Staff |
| 2026-11-27 | The rings of summoning affinity and natural attunement (RS 1): twelve [[rangoric-srd/magic-items/rings/Ring of Summoning Affinity, Aeon\|rings of summoning affinity]] (aeon, agathion, angel, archon, asura, daemon, div, inevitable, kyton, protean, psychopomp, qlippoth) and three [[rangoric-srd/magic-items/rings/Ring of Natural Attunement, Drake\|rings of natural attunement]] (drake, kami, leshy), each adding its creatures to the _summon monster_ or _summon nature’s ally_ lists and summoning one of them once a day; the angel ring's "cassian" is the cassisian, the archon ring's creator must be lawful good, and the kami ring's inscription names no language. All fifteen join More Rings' any-wearer table by price. | `magic-items/rings` (15 new), [[rangoric-srd/magic-items/Magic Potions, Rings, and Rods\|Magic Potions, Rings, and Rods]] (see [[RS Batch 1 Changes]]) | — | [[Summoning Rings]] RS 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Intimidation states its aftermath once: the two sentences on what happens when intimidation ends become one (unfriendly, or hostile if already unfriendly, and may report you), and the pointer to Persuasion drops "above". | [[rangoric-srd/skills/Intimidation\|Intimidation]] | | Intimidation's drawbacks |
| 2026-11-27 | World building (WB 2): Pathfinder's setting names leave the SRD. The First World is the [[rangoric-srd/flavor/the-fey-realm/The Fey Realm\|Fey Realm]] (Fey Gate, Enhanced Gnome Magic, Fey-Guarded, seven spell lists); the Great Beyond is "the planes" (Upasunda), and Scholar of the Great Beyond is renamed [[rangoric-srd/traits/faith-traits/Scholar of the Planes\|Scholar of the Planes]] (Background Generator Steps 1 and 2 too); Race Builder Traits' "Deep Stalker (Darklands Stalker)" is "Deep Stalker"; Kostchtchie is renamed [[rangoric-srd/monsters/demon/Koschei\|Koschei]] (his note, seven abilities, Demon, All Monsters), without the old name's pronunciation. 38 NPC clerics and paladins name their god from [[rangoric-srd/divine/Pantheons\|Pantheons]] ("9 levels of cleric of Nergal"); the Death Priest serves "the god", the Vivisectionist Cleric and Pious Guard "the goddess". | [[WB Batch 2 Changes]] | | [[World Building for Pathfinder Names]] WB 2 |
| 2026-11-27 | World building (WB 1): the SRD gets its own gods. New [[rangoric-srd/divine/Pantheons\|Pantheons]] lists 89 gods of Egyptian, Babylonian and Sumerian, Norse, Greek and Roman, Japanese and Aztec myth (alignment, portfolio, domains), and the fiends and other powers the SRD names with where each name comes from; Divine links it. Background Generator Step 3's deity paragraph points at it and gains Table: Deities (20 gods, each granting a faith trait). New religion trait [[rangoric-srd/traits/faith-traits/Wrecking Wrath\|Wrecking Wrath]] (Apep's row). "Check notes" now fails a changed note that names a Pathfinder setting name. | [[WB Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[World Building for Pathfinder Names]] WB 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 9): no note carries `based-on:` or `change:` ("Check notes" fails either); source links are kept in the workshop, not in SRD notes. Rangoric SRD's properties and how-it-gets-built text say so. | [[NO Batch 9 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 9 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 8): the remaining folders' notes (rules, powers, flavor, classes, prestige classes, skills, psionics, epic, design notes, actions, combat, divine) and Rangoric SRD and the Tag Register drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 8 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 8 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 7): race, unearthed arcana and trait notes drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 7 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 7 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 6): equipment notes drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 6 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 6 |
| 2026-11-27 | Linnorm to Lindwurm (RL 1): the linnorm is now the lindwurm. `monsters/linnorm/` becomes `monsters/lindwurms/`, and Lindwurm, Cairn, Crag, Fjord, Ice, Taiga, Tarn and Tor Lindwurm, the Lindwurm (Mutated Bloodline) and the seven Lindwurm Death Curse rage powers are renamed, with their links and mentions in Fated Champion, Wildblooded, Bloodlines, Draconic Bloodline, Rage Powers, Summoner, All Monsters, Monster Roles, Monsters K to L, Nycar, Water Orm, Frost Giant and Death Curse (29 notes); "Check notes" fails "linnorm" in any note but Legal and the Changelog. | [[RL Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Linnorm to Lindwurm]] RL 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 5): feats drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 5 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 5 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 4): spells drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 4 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 4 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 3): class options drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 3 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 3 |
| 2026-11-27 | Product Identity cleanup (PN 1): the nagaji, samsaran, suli and wayang stay out of Rangoric, and renamed entries keep no Product Identity alias. Race of Adopted Sibling gives each excluded row to the row above (Merfolk 85–86, Ratfolk 89–90, Strix 91–92, Vishkanya 99–100); Infuse Self's list drops aphorite, duskwalker, ganzi and suli; Race Builder Traits loses Samsaran Magic; Explorer's Kit, Tarot Deck, Horns of the Undead Tyrant and Deepdark Goggles lose their old aliases; Wondrous Items by Slot links "Deepdark goggles" and "Orb of the foul wasteland". | [[PN Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Product Identity Cleanup]] PN 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 2): magic items drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys. | [[NO Batch 2 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 2 |
| 2026-11-27 | No Based-On (NO 1): monsters drop the `based-on:` and `change:` frontmatter keys; the README's Note rules no longer require them. | [[NO Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[No Based-On]] NO 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Old fiend names (OF 1): the text around the old fiend names keeps no Pathfinder setting. Fire Cleric and Fire Diabolist drop "It is a cleric of Asmodeus.", the Asmodean Demon Hunter is "Raised in a church of Asmodeus or another archdevil", Kostchtchie confronts "the witch Baba Yaga" (linked to the Fey Realm's Baba Yaga), Dagon's qlippoth is linked, and the Scepter of Pazuzu "can be broken by a redeemed demon lord" with no towers clause. Abyssal Resurrection and Demon Lord Traits lose the stray "the" before each demon lord. | [[OF Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Old Fiend Names]] OF 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Book names in file names (BF 1): no file name carries a source book. The armor abilities Floating and the weapon abilities Burning, Dispelling, Impact and Sundering that had "(Arms & Armor)", and the spell Fool’s Gold that had "(Faeries)", become "(Alternate)" beside the clean notes of those names; the armor ability Spell Storing becomes Spell Storing (Armor Special Ability) and the alchemical Philosopher’s Stone becomes Philosopher’s Stone (Alchemical Item). 29 links in 16 notes follow, and a link that showed the old name shows the new one. | [[BF Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Book Names in File Names]] BF 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Protean language (LG 1): Linguistics' language table gains Adlet, Cyclops, Garuda, Grimlock, Kasatha, Maenad, Protean (proteans, chaotic neutral outsiders), Sahuagin, Shae, Sphinx (sphinxes, maftets), Xeph and Yithian, so every language a note names has a row; Unintentional Linguist's list links to the table; the god stealer, master storycrafter, water merchant and vaultbreaker speak Elven and the barkeep and master universalist Dwarven (were "Elf" and "Dwarf"); "Check notes" fails a changed note naming a language that isn't in the table. | [[LG Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Protean Language]] LG 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Two-skill feats (TS 1): replaced the +2/+2 feats Bodily Awareness, Brewmaster, Oracular Intuition and Sure and Fleet with Reciprocal Knowledge, which now takes any two skills (+4 in a skill with 10 ranks, no prerequisite); Brewmaster's poison perk is its first pairing. The Apothecary's 3rd-level bonus feat and Diagnosis name Reciprocal Knowledge (Medicine and Profession (apothecary)), the buccaneer gains Reciprocal Knowledge (Acrobatics and Athletics) for Sea Legs, the fungal nymph has Reciprocal Knowledge (Fieldcraft and Medicine) for Self-Sufficient, and "Check notes" fails a new +2/+2 two-skill feat. | [[TS Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Two-Skill Feats]] TS 1 |
| 2026-11-27 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 7): the Spells and Powers Note Rules list the allowed descriptors, and "Check notes" fails a note whose chaotic, lawful, good or evil tags don't match its descriptors. | [[CL Batch 7 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 7 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 6): 70 spells have one spelling of each descriptor (Mind-Affecting, Language-Dependent, See text), and subschools in the descriptor slot move to the subschool. | [[CL Batch 6 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 6 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 5): 75 spells have one spelling of each descriptor (Mind-Affecting, Language-Dependent, See text), and subschools in the descriptor slot move to the subschool. | [[CL Batch 5 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 5 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 4): 75 spells have one spelling of each descriptor (Mind-Affecting, Language-Dependent, See text), Fire Shield lists Fire and Cold, and subschools in the descriptor slot move to the subschool. | [[CL Batch 4 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 4 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 3): the last 49 spells with an alignment descriptor get its tag, 26 more get one spelling of their descriptors (Mind-Affecting, Language-Dependent, See text), and the Tag Register lists the chaotic, lawful, good and evil tags. | [[CL Batch 3 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 3 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 2): the chaos and law descriptors are chaotic and lawful (Cacophony, Shard of Chaos, Bestow Grace of the Champion, and the text of Cleric, Druid, Hunter, the Chaos and Law domains, Cleric Domains, Nameless Dread and Designing Spells); 67 spells, powers and occult rituals get chaotic, lawful, good or evil tags matching their descriptors. | [[CL Batch 2 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 2 |
| 2026-11-26 | Chaos and law descriptors (CL 1): Magic Overview's descriptor list adds curse, disease, pain, poison and shadow, and a new paragraph says the chaotic, evil, good and lawful descriptors are also tags. | [[CL Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Chaos and Law Descriptors]] CL 1 |
| 2026-11-26 | Small text fixes (ST 1): doubled full stops, doubled words, stray spaces before punctuation and a doubled comma fixed in 59 notes; epic spells' Components lines read "V, S, XP" and "DF"; the Dream, Enchantment and Illusion domains no longer name a book; Equipment Weapons drops its sentence about the deleted Weapon Group feats; both witch archetypes label the hex "nightmares"; the Gholdako's "would-be thieves", the Rune Caster's caster level and Flurry of Stars' "full attack" reworded. | 2026-11-26 | Saving throws become defenses: Saving Throws says each save is a defense (Fortitude, Reflex and Will Defense, 10 + the save bonus) that whoever makes an effect attacks, with the effect's attack bonus (a spell's level + casting ability modifier, a monster ability's 1/2 HD + ability modifier, the old DC − 10), no natural 1 or 20 against a defense, repeated saves as repeated attacks, rerolls as stacking disadvantage, lowering a defense, and items harmed when an attack beats a defense by 10 or more. The save wording of Combat, Magic Overview (the Defense entry), Power Description Format, Basics, Afflictions (the Attack line), Traps (sample traps and design tables), Special Abilities and Conditions, Monsters (the Defenses line), Improving Monsters and Spell-Like Abilities is rewritten to match. | [[rangoric-srd/rules/Saving Throws\|Saving Throws]], [[rangoric-srd/combat/Combat\|Combat]], [[rangoric-srd/spells/Magic Overview\|Magic Overview]], [[rangoric-srd/psionics/Power Description Format\|Power Description Format]], [[rangoric-srd/rules/Basics\|Basics]], [[rangoric-srd/rules/Afflictions\|Afflictions]], [[rangoric-srd/rules/Traps\|Traps]], [[rangoric-srd/rules/Special Abilities and Conditions\|Special Abilities and Conditions]], [[rangoric-srd/monsters/Monsters\|Monsters]], [[rangoric-srd/monsters/Improving Monsters\|Improving Monsters]], [[rangoric-srd/monsters/abilities/Spell-Like Abilities\|Spell-Like Abilities]], [[DF Batch 1 Changes]] | | [[Saving Throws as Defenses]] DF 1 |
| 2026-11-25 | Mysticism leftovers: NPC stat blocks (NPC Gallery and NPC Codex, 135 notes) get Spellcraft back as Mysticism from their sources, as in MY 4. Text that a source gives as Spellcraft or Psicraft but that named Arcana or Psionics now names Mysticism (16 spells, 3 psionics notes, Detect Psionics, feats, magic items, archetypes, the Sage bloodline and Child of Yog-Sothoth). Mysticism joins the class skills of Reflecting Master, Divine Emissary, Cloistered Cleric, Cult Leader, Sacred Fist, Eldritch Raider, and the ghost, lich and skeletal champion templates. Disruptive Recall needs Mysticism 2 ranks, Improved Metapsionics and Spontaneous Domain Access Mysticism 27. | [[MY Batch 5 Changes]] | [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (3.5)]] | [[Mysticism]] |

| 2026-11-25 | Mysticism in monster stat blocks: each block's Spellcraft or Psicraft is back as Mysticism, from its source (401 skill lists in 238 notes, with their `skills` and `skill-ranks`). A Knowledge skill (Arcana, Theology, Nature, Psionics) that held only Spellcraft is renamed Mysticism; one that took the higher of Knowledge and Spellcraft goes back to the Knowledge total. 3.5 blocks recomputed on conversion get Mysticism shifted like their other skills. Dragon age table rows without a source follow the nearest sourced age. Magical Knack and Ogre Mage name Mysticism as a class skill; Soulbound Shell's Skill Focus is Mysticism. | [[MY Batch 4 Changes]] | [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (3.5)]] | [[Mysticism]] |
| 2026-11-25 | Mysticism everywhere else: "Magic skill" checks, ranks and links in feats, magic items, equipment, spells, rules, unearthed-arcana, traits, divine, epic, monsters and flavor notes are Mysticism. Feat prerequisites "N ranks in a Magic skill" are Mysticism N ranks; the 15 epic feats with "Arcana, Nature or Theology N ranks" are Mysticism N ranks; the seven epic item creation feats get Mysticism beside Arcana at the same number. Epic spells per day stay a tenth of Arcana, Theology or Nature ranks, and the casting check is Mysticism. Occult rituals: a printed Spellcraft check is a Mysticism check again (Avoidance Ward, Natural Reclamation, Psychic Shielding, Ritual Exorcism, Veil Structure), replacing the theme rule. Cosmic Descryer and High Proselytizer get Mysticism as a class skill; Kaiein's requirement is Arcana 2, Theology 2, Mysticism 7. | [[MY Batch 3 Changes]] | [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (3.5)]] | [[Mysticism]] |
| 2026-11-25 | Mysticism on the class skill lists: the "Magic Skill" line leaves every class note, and Mysticism (Int) is a class skill for the 86 classes and prestige classes that cast spells or manifest powers, or whose source list had Spellcraft or Psicraft. Spellcraft and Psicraft requirements come back as Mysticism ranks (source minus 3): Archmage 12, Dragon Mage 10, Blood Mage 5, Spellsinger 5, Metamind 1, Psion Uncarnate 5. Class options and race text say Mysticism where they said a Magic skill; the psychic bloodline, onmyoji and urban ranger lose their "Magic skill is X instead" sentences. | [[MY Batch 2 Changes]] | [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (3.5)]], [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Psicraft\|Psicraft (3.5)]] | [[Mysticism]] |
| 2026-11-25 | New skill Mysticism (Int, Core, trained only, tags magic and psionic): one skill identifies arcane, divine, nature and psychic spells and psionic powers, with the identification uses, Advanced Uses and unlocks that were Identifying Magic's, and is the check for casting epic spells. Identifying Magic is replaced by it. Arcana, Theology, Nature, Psionics and Psychic are plain Knowledge skills again (lore and creatures; identifying an effect is Mysticism) and keep only the Knowledge unlocks. Skill lists, Psionic Skills, Tag Register, Knowledge, Skills Overview, Craft (alchemy), Appraise, Emulation and Perception point to Mysticism. | [[rangoric-srd/skills/Mysticism\|Mysticism]], [[MY Batch 1 Changes]] | [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (3.5)]], [[fantasy srd 3.5/skills/Psicraft\|Psicraft (3.5)]], [[path srd 3.5/skills/Spellcraft\|Spellcraft (PF1)]] | [[Mysticism]] |

| 2026-11-16 | Pathfinder's alternate favored class options come in as class bonuses of a race (favored class itself stays dropped). PC Races gets a Class Bonuses section (`^class-bonuses`): a race's bonus for a class is gained for every level in that class, automatically and in every listed class; bonuses stack, fractions (+1/2, +1/3, +1/4, +1/6) add up and round down, printed limits stop a bonus, choices are made at each level, a companion's bonus carries to its replacement, prestige classes don't count, and the monk's and summoner's apply to the unchained versions too. The dwarf's and elf's 29 bonuses each are notes in `races/class-bonuses` (`Dwarf Fighter (Class Bonus)` …) with `race` and `class`, the printed text with only the selection wording changed, skills translated (Disable Device → Engineering, Knowledge (dungeoneering) → Dungeoneering, Survival → Fieldcraft) and the dwarf rogue's trap sense read as danger sense. `Class Bonuses.base` has an All view and a view per race and per class; the Dwarf and Elf notes embed theirs in a Class Bonuses section after Subtypes, and 31 class notes embed theirs in a **Class Bonuses** paragraph (`^<class>-class-bonuses`) after their archetypes or last class feature. | [[rangoric-srd/races/PC Races\|PC Races]], [[rangoric-srd/races/Dwarf\|Dwarf]], [[rangoric-srd/races/Elf\|Elf]], [[rangoric-srd/races/class-bonuses/Class Bonuses.base\|Class Bonuses base]] (see [[CB Batch 1 Changes]]) | [[path srd 3.5/races/Dwarves (Advanced Race Guide)\|PF1]], [[path srd 3.5/races/Elves (Advanced Race Guide)\|PF1]], [[path srd 3.5/rules/advanced-class-guide/hybrid-classes/Racial Favored Class Options\|PF1]] | [[Favored Class Options]] CB 1 |

| 2026-11-15 | Poison use, treating afflictions, and items, powers and hazards in the affliction block. Poison use is stated once, in Afflictions, and now covers attacking with a poisoned weapon: the alchemist's, ninja's and investigator's (poison lore), the assassin's and blackguard's, the slayer talent, the Poison Use racial trait, the race builder's Poison Use, the derro's and the frost giant jarl's point to it, as does touch injection. | 2026-11-10 | Groundwork for the Pathfinder races: 16 humanoid subtypes in Monster Types (catfolk, changeling, dhampir, dwarf, elf, gnome, grippli, halfling, human, kitsune, orc, ratfolk, strix, tengu, vanara, vishkanya), the amphibious special quality and negative energy affinity (Pathfinder's universal monster rules) and positive energy affinity (wording drafted), which the aquatic subtype, the monster Merfolk and Deathly Being point to; a "Type: Humanoid (x)" line on the seven core races; PC Races says most characters know Common and a race's note lists it when they do, while races that aren't human or close to it (feral, xenophobic) may not, Common being a bonus language for almost anyone (wording drafted) and gets a Level Adjustment section pointing to Monsters as Races; 10 languages in Linguistics (Aboleth, Aklo, Boggard, Catfolk, Drow Sign Language, Grippli, Strix, Tengu, Vanaran, Vishkanya); hold breath has one home in Wilderness (Suffocation, 2 rounds per point of Constitution), which Drowning and Athletics point to, so swimmers hold their breath twice as long as Athletics said, and the home takes Athletics' rule that standard and full-round actions use up an extra round; races and creature types stay separate (Rangoric SRD decision ticked). | [[rangoric-srd/monsters/Monster Types, Sybtypes, and Special Abilities\|Monster Types]], [[rangoric-srd/races/PC Races\|PC Races]], 7 race notes, [[rangoric-srd/skills/Linguistics\|Linguistics]], [[rangoric-srd/skills/Athletics\|Athletics]], [[rangoric-srd/rules/Wilderness, Weather, and Environment\|Wilderness]] (see [[RT Batch 26 Changes]]) | [[path srd 3.5/rules/bestiary/Universal Monster Rules\|Universal Monster Rules (PF1)]], ARG races | Race Topics overnight plan RT 26 (S3–S5, S11, S12, S14; Wilson 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-10) |
| 2026-11-10 | Four weapons from Pathfinder for the races: dogslicer (martial, light, fragile) and horsechopper (martial, two-handed, reach, trip) for the goblin, the elven curve blade (exotic, two-handed) for the tengu, the blowgun and its darts (simple, ranged) for the vishkanya, each with its table row, description and Pathfinder weapon group; an Eastern Weapons list (kama, katana, kusarigama, naginata, nunchaku, sai, shuriken, siangham, wakizashi) for the tengu's Exotic Weapon Training, to become a weapon tag later. The light descriptor needed no change (every light spell already has it). | [[rangoric-srd/equipment/Equipment Weapons\|Equipment Weapons]] (see [[RT Batch 27 Changes]]) | [[path srd 3.5/rules/ultimate-equipment/arms-and-armor/Weapons (Arms And Armor)\|Weapons (Arms and Armor, PF1)]], PF1 Core Rulebook | Race Topics overnight plan RT 27 (Wilson 2026-11-09 and 2026-11-10) |

| 2026-11-10 | Feint and demoralize move to Pathfinder's fixed DCs. Feint (Combat Special Attacks, now the rule's one home): Deception against DC 10 + the target's base attack bonus + its Wisdom modifier, or 10 + its Sense Motive bonus if it's trained and that's higher; Deception's Feinting in Combat and Action line point to it. Demoralize (Intimidation): DC 10 + the target's Hit Dice + its Wisdom modifier, shaken 1 round + 1 per 5 over, within 30 feet and able to see and hear you; Try Again's +5 also raises the DC. Intimidation's influence use stays opposed. | [[FD Batch 1 Changes]] | 3 notes | [[Feint and Demoralize]] |
| 2026-11-10 | The feats refitted to the fixed feint and demoralize DCs: Social and Inspiring Bravery add the bravery bonus to the DCs, Deadhand Style and Initiate raise the Intimidation DC by 4 and 8, Indomitable Mountain Style adds +4 to the DC to feint you, Fox Insight's base attack bonus stands in for Sense Motive ranks in the feint DC (no longer counting twice); Confabulist's Normal points to Deception's Try Again. The other feats, class options and spells naming feint or demoralize already fit. | [[FD Batch 2 Changes]] | 7 notes | [[Feint and Demoralize]] |
| 2026-11-10 | Tumbling goes against CMD (Pathfinder): Acrobatics to move through a threatened square is against the opponent's CMD, through its space CMD + 5, +2 per additional opponent avoided in 1 round, and full speed adds 10 to the DC; Pathfinder's limits replace 3.5's armor-and-equipment line (no tumbling past foes with speed reduced by a medium or heavy load or armor, unless an ability allows full speed), tumbling while prone takes a full-round action for 5 feet at DC +5, and a failed tumble through an enemy's space loses the move action. Combat Maneuvers names this use of CMD; Combat Movement's Tumbling no longer needs training; Indomitable Mountain Style counts you as one size larger for that CMD. | [[FD Batch 3 Changes]] | 4 notes | [[Feint and Demoralize]] |

| 2026-10-22 | Concentration is no longer a skill. | 2026-10-22 | Psionic focus has its own section in Psionics: gaining it is manifester level (or level in a psionic class that doesn't manifest, like the soulknife) + Con against DC 20, or a Meditation check. Both were in the 3.5 psionic Concentration text that the conversion had lost | [[rangoric-srd/psionics/Psionics#Psionic Focus\|Psionics]] | 3.5 `psionicSkills.html` (raw) | Restored, under the new check; see [[Working Folder/Done/plans/Skill List Rework\|Skill List Rework]] |
